Protocols
=========

BBSes grouped by detected connection protocol.
A BBS may appear in multiple groups.

.. figure:: _static/plots/bbs_protocols.png
   :align: center
   :width: 800px
   :alt: Pie chart showing the distribution of connection protocols across all BBSes.
